Map of Canada – The 45th Parliament (PDF format)

Definitions and Qualifications

Qualifications

Issue Description
Redistribution As a part of the 2022 redistribution of electoral districts, polling division boundaries were changed. Therefore, results from individual electoral districts and polling divisions that appear in poll-by-poll results for the 2025 general election are not geographically equivalent to similarly named electoral districts and polling divisions that appear in earlier elections.

For more information, see the Redistribution of Federal Electoral Districts 2022 page on elections.ca
Votes Greater Than Elector Count At times, the total number of votes at a poll will exceed the number of voters on the list of electors. This can happen for various administrative reasons, including missing registration certificates or when electors from one poll vote at another poll using a transfer certificate. Elections Canada is reviewing these occurrences following the election to better understand what took place and would transfer cases to the office of the Commissioner of Canada Elections for investigation if a potential violation of the Canada Elections Act were detected.
Results Variation Election results are recorded by election workers in front of witnesses (candidate representatives or other qualified electors and validated in front of witnesses after election day. At times, data entry errors do occur. The validation process is designed to identify and correct any errors that may have occurred during preliminary reporting. Under the Canada Elections Act, the results are only official once validated by the returning officer. Elections Canada examines cases where we believe that a data entry error may have occurred to improve processes in the future and make sure that such errors did not in any way affect the overall result of the vote.

Format 1 CSV Field Descriptions

pollbypoll_bureauparbureau format (format 1)
Field Name Description
Electoral District Number The electoral district number.
Electoral District Name The name of the electoral district.
Polling Division Number The number assigned to the polling division, for example: 3, 45A, 48-3, 601.
Polling Division Name A name that generally represents the locality of the polling division boundary.
[Candidate 1 name] The number of valid votes for the first candidate on the ballot for this polling division only.
[Candidate 2 name] The number of valid votes for the second candidate on the ballot for this polling division only.
[Candidate 3 name] The number of valid votes for the third candidate on the ballot for this polling division only.
[Candidate 4 name] The number of valid votes for the fourth candidate on the ballot for this polling division only.
... ...
[Candidate 11 name] The number of valid votes for the eleventh candidate (if any) on the ballot for this polling division only.
Rejected Ballots The number of rejected ballots for this polling division.
Total Votes The total number of ballots counted for this polling division.
Electors The number of electors on the list of electors for this polling division.

Format 2 CSV Field Descriptions

pollresults_resultatsbureau format (format 2)
Field Name Description
Electoral District Number The electoral district number.
Electoral District Name_English The English name of the electoral district.
Electoral District Name_French The French name of the electoral district.
Polling Division Number The number assigned to the polling division, or subsection thereof.
for example: 3, 45A, 48-3, 601.
Polling Division Name A name that generally represents the locality of the polling division boundary.
Void Poll Indicator Indicates that a poll exists but has no electors.
No Poll Held Indicator Indicates that the returning officer intended to hold this poll, but unforeseen circumstances prevented it.
Combined with No.   "Combined" polling divisions are those where electors from different polling divisions placed their ballots in the same ballot box so that service to electors would be more efficient. This term may also be used in cases where votes from two ballot boxes were counted together; or where all electors in one poll voted for the same candidate and their ballots were reported combined with another poll in order to maintain the secrecy of the vote.
Rejected Ballots for Polling Station The number of rejected ballots at this poll.
Electors for Poll The number of electors on the list of electors for this polling.
Candidate’s Family Name The family name of the candidate.
Candidate’s Middle Name The middle name of the candidate.
Candidate’s First Name The first name of the candidate.
Political Affiliation Name_English The short-form English name of the candidate’s political affiliation.
Political Affiliation Name_French The short-form French name of the candidate’s political affiliation.
Incumbent Indicator “Y” if candidate was the incumbent, “N” otherwise.
Elected Candidate Indicator “Y” if candidate was elected, “N” otherwise.
Candidate VoteCount The number of valid votes the candidate obtained at this poll.
